# Deferred Work

Tracks findings deferred from code reviews and other quality gates. Each section is dated and references the originating workflow.

## Story 2.2 — resolutions + new deferrals (tone-guide + vocabulary enforcement process author-commit, 2026-06-20)

Closure-language posture per [[feedback_closure_language_precision]]: **Closed by [edit]** only where this story produced the artifact; everything else is **Resolved via explicit deferral** (gap intentional, rationale + re-trigger recorded). Story 2.2 ships the **human layer** of tone enforcement above the Story 1.17 `microcopy` automated floor: the `docs/tone-guide.md` + `docs/tone-review-checklist.md` process, plus its **runtime teeth** — a pure fail-closed `evaluateToneReviewGate` (`packages/domain/src/tone-review/`) + a Fastify `requireToneReviewSignoff` pre-handler (`apps/api/src/modules/tone-review/`) enforcing a NON-AUTHOR sign-off before member-visible-copy publish, mapped to HTTP 409 `tone-review-required`, with a dedicated `tone_review.*` audit seam through the Story 1.10 writer. Scope was LOCKED at create-story: 2.2 ships the gate **mechanism**, not the persistence (no member-visible-copy publish endpoint exists yet — the first, Niyamavali publish, is Story 2.4). Per [[feedback_record_unattested_no_backfill]], every deferral below carries an explicit re-trigger so the commitment cannot decay un-gated.

**No inherited item to Close:** a grep of this file for `tone-review` / `tone_review` / FR-69 routing returns nothing — Story 2.2 inherits no binding deferred-work entry. It only *adds* new deferrals (and Closes its own binding deliverables). The `microcopy` member-register / `copy_globs` deferrals from Story 1.17 + 2.1 are NOT fired here (this story does not widen the lint — the tone-guide doc lives in `docs/`, outside the bounded `apps/admin` scan scope).

**Closed by [edit] (Story 2.2):**

- **Story 2.2 binding deliverables (AC1–AC6)** — **Closed by [edit].** Landed: `docs/tone-guide.md` (the four named dimensions — voice; per-surface register mapped to architecture §4.15 actor-class register; prohibited frames; grief-context modulation — sourced + inline-cited from the UX spec + architecture, with the explicit "Automated floor vs. human check" §5); `docs/tone-review-checklist.md` (one item per AC1 dimension + grief-context gate + non-author-reviewer attestation + the publish-routing process); the pure domain gate (`packages/domain/src/tone-review/{errors,gate,index}.ts` — `ToneReviewRequiredError` + `toErrorResponse` 409 projector + `evaluateToneReviewGate` fail-closed evaluator; wired into `packages/domain/src/index.ts` as the `toneReview` namespace + top-level `ToneReviewRequiredError`; 5 vitest cases); the API adapter (`apps/api/src/modules/tone-review/index.ts` — `requireToneReviewSignoff` pre-handler + the dedicated `ToneReviewAuditSink` seam + `toneReviewEventToAuditInput` mapper + `recordToneReviewSignoff` helper; seam wired into `AppDeps`/`createDeps`/test-deps mirroring `auditSink`); the error-mapping 409 branch (`apps/api/src/middleware/error-mapping/index.ts`); and the teeth suite (`apps/api/tests/unit/tone-review.test.ts` — 11 cases: block-without-signoff + author==reviewer + valid-non-author + async resolver + loud-500 + never-throw + audit-input mapping + 409 mapping). ADR-0019 authored (drafted). gate-inventory Category B row added. See ADR-0019.

**Resolved via explicit deferral (Story 2.2):**

- **Sign-off PERSISTENCE — the durable which-artifact-was-reviewed record** — **Resolved via explicit deferral.** There is no member-visible-copy publish endpoint at Story 2.2, and the reviewed-artifact identity is polymorphic across surfaces that do not exist yet (a `clause_version_id` from 2.3, a future `news_post_id`, a `push_template_id`…). Manufacturing a generic `copy_review_signoffs` table now would violate the "no speculative store" discipline. The gate evaluator operates on an **injected** sign-off record; the pre-handler resolves it via `opts.resolveSignoff`. **Re-trigger:** **Story 2.4** Niyamavali publish — which already commits to recording tone-reviewer attribution + `clause_version_id` (epics.md L1485) — owns the first concrete persistence + the resolver implementation.
- **Gate CONSUMER WIRING — mounting `requireToneReviewSignoff` on a real publish route** — **Resolved via explicit deferral.** The pre-handler is installed + unit-tested but mounted by no route (gate-inventory Category B). **Re-trigger:** **Story 2.4** adds `requireToneReviewSignoff` to the Niyamavali publish `preHandler` chain (the established land-once-primitive → consumer-wires-in-with-teeth pattern).
- **Future governed copy surfaces — News/Blog · T&C · push-notification templates · helpdesk macros** — **Resolved via explicit deferral.** The tone-review checklist names these as governed surfaces, but their publish endpoints + resource-specific review permissions do not exist yet (only `niyamavali.review` is seeded). Each surface uses its own review key, added by the story that ships it — no generic `copy.review` key is manufactured. **Re-trigger (per surface, at its owning story):** News/Blog → its Epic 2/later story; T&C → Story 2.6 (T&C version pinning); push templates → their notification story; helpdesk macros → the Epic 10 helpdesk story. Each mounts `requireToneReviewSignoff` with its own resolver + review-permission enforcement.

## Story 2.1 — resolutions + new deferrals (i18n centralized utility + bilingual surface contract author-commit, 2026-06-20)

Closure-language posture per [[feedback_closure_language_precision]]: **Closed by [edit]** only where this story produced the artifact; everything else is **Resolved via explicit deferral** (gap intentional, rationale + re-trigger recorded). Story 2.1 lands `packages/i18n` — the centralized i18n utility enforcing the architectural-freeze row-10 bilingual contract ONCE at the utility level (member-visible surfaces Hindi-primary; admin surfaces English-primary; member strings carry Hindi parity verified by a build-time CI gate). Delivered: the `Locale` primitive (defined locally; value-aligned-not-symbol-identical with the domain `localeEnum`, PariwarId-at-1.7 precedent) + `getLocale(ctx)` precedence resolver (framework-agnostic — imports no HTTP type) + the React `LocaleProvider`/`useLocale`/`useT` toggle context (`react` an OPTIONAL peer dep, not a module-level singleton); the `t(key, params, options)` resolver with `{varName}` single-brace interpolation + loud-by-default missing-key/namespace/param throws; per-domain `locales/{hi,en}/{domain}.json` storage + a static-import catalog; the surface-classification registry (`locales/classification.json`, declarative-by-namespace not app-path, member-facing default); the build-time parity gate (pure `scripts/lib.ts` + impure `scripts/check-parity.ts`, naming file+key on failure, teeth proven end-to-end) wired as the `i18n:check-parity` turbo task → `i18n-parity` ci.yml job → `scripts/ci-local.sh` registration; and the AC6 numeral/currency utilities. Scope was LOCKED at create-story: AC1–AC5 (binding epics ACs) + AC6 (the deferred-work L22 re-trigger); the broader §4328 module set is deferred (below).

**Inherited item Closed (Story 2.1):**

- **`packages/i18n` numeral utilities (`toHindiNumeral` / `toGregorianNumeral` / `formatCurrency`) — the Story 1.17 L22 deferral** — **Closed by [edit].** Story 2.1 IS the named re-trigger ("Story 2.1 lands `packages/i18n`"). The three functions landed in `packages/i18n/src/number.ts` + `src/currency.ts` (Devanagari↔Latin conversion; `₹` with Indian lakh/crore grouping), with the amendment-A2 operational-vs-ceremonial split encoded as the documented contract (operational data → Latin even on memorial pages; ceremonial Devanagari prose → Hindi numerals permitted; never mixed at one hierarchy level) in code comments + README + tests. The 1.17 microcopy gate's "inline numeral formatting outside the utility" note is now anchored: `packages/i18n` is the sanctioned home (ci.yml microcopy job comment updated). The original L22 entry is annotated inline below.

**Closed by [edit] (Story 2.1):**

- **Story 2.1 binding deliverables (AC1–AC6)** — **Closed by [edit].** Landed: `packages/i18n/src/{locale,resolver,number,currency,classification,catalog,index}.ts` + `locales/{hi,en}/common.json` + `locales/classification.json` + `scripts/{lib,check-parity}.ts` + `README.md` + the 42-test vitest suite (locale precedence incl. hi fallback; resolver + interpolation + loud throws; numeral round-trips; currency lakh/crore + decimals + negative + hi-Devanagari + non-finite throw; classification parse/resolve/declare; parity pure-core hit/miss/member-vs-admin/empty-no-op). CI wiring: `turbo.json` `i18n:check-parity` task + root `i18n:check` passthrough + `i18n-parity` ci.yml job (between `tokens-theme-check` and `pii-scrape`) + `scripts/ci-local.sh` registration (static-job count corrected to 15 — the header had drifted to a stale "13" after cadence-check (AI-1) landed as the 14th without a header bump; i18n-parity is the 15th). Green-with-teeth: `common` defaults member-facing and is parity-enforced now; teeth proven end-to-end (en-only key → exit 1 naming it → removed → exit 0, tree clean). See ADR-0018.

**Resolved via explicit deferral (Story 2.1):**

- **Broader architecture §4328 i18n modules — `date.ts` / `relative-time.ts` / `pluralize.ts` / `actor-class-register.ts` / `per-pariwar/` string overlays** — **Resolved via explicit deferral.** §4328 sketches a fuller i18n module set; Story 2.1 deliberately lands only the AC-bound surface (resolver + locale + classification + parity) plus the three named numeral/currency functions (the L22 re-trigger). No surface consumes the remaining modules yet; building them now is premature plumbing (the "scaffolding with no consumer" trap). **Re-triggers (per module, at first consuming surface):** `date.ts` / `relative-time.ts` → the first surface rendering member-facing dates/relative times (Epic 3 member lifecycle / life-events panel); `pluralize.ts` → the first member-facing pluralized count string; `actor-class-register.ts` → the first per-actor-class copy variance (architecture §2867-2870, per-state copy + actor-class register); `per-pariwar/` overlays → second-Pariwar provisioning needing per-tenant string overrides.
- **Server-consumer React-coupling — `@twt/i18n/react` subpath split** — **Resolved via explicit deferral.** The client hooks (`LocaleProvider`/`useLocale`/`useT`) are re-exported from the package root (per the story's single-index design), so importing `@twt/i18n` pulls `react` into the module graph. At v1 there are NO consumers, so this is inert (`react` is an optional peer dep; the server-safe core — `t`, `getLocale`, numerals — is otherwise framework-agnostic). **Re-trigger:** the first non-React server consumer (e.g. `apps/api` for API copy/locale layering, architecture L1833-1834) imports the package — at that point split the React hooks into a `@twt/i18n/react` subpath export so the core loads without `react`.
- **Broad member-surface parity enforcement footprint** — **Resolved via explicit deferral.** `classification.json` `namespaces` is empty (no admin-only namespace yet) and the 1.17 microcopy `scope.copy_globs` is NOT widened at 2.1 (no member surface ships locale strings yet — the L24 broad-member-enforcement re-trigger is intentionally NOT fired here). Parity teeth currently bite the `common` member-facing namespace; they grow domain-by-domain as Epic 2+ member surfaces add `locales/**` strings, data-driven, no gate code change. **Re-trigger:** Epic 2+ member surfaces populate additional `locales/{hi,en}/{domain}.json` (and, for the microcopy register, member `copy_globs` per the existing L24 deferral).

## Story 1.17 — resolutions + new deferrals (design-system foundation author-commit, 2026-06-16)

Closure-language posture per [[feedback_closure_language_precision]]: **Closed by [edit]** only where this story produced the artifact; everything else is **Resolved via explicit deferral** (gap intentional, rationale + trigger recorded). Story 1.17 lands the design-system foundation: the hand-rolled `@twt/tokens` registry (color/spacing/typography/border) + a deterministic Tailwind v4 `@theme` generator with an FM-4 byte-compare sync gate + the subsumption of admin's DD-1 inline `@theme` + the repo-global `scripts/microcopy/` vocabulary (UX-DR71) / numeral (UX-DR73 / amendment A2) / FM-14 #2 magic-number gate. Two epic-phrasing corrections are baked in: tokens home = `packages/tokens` (not `packages/ui`); "FM-1..FM-14 forensic-microcopy rules" = the seven UX-spec Failure-Mode commitments (the gate realizes the automatable subset; ADR-0016 records the mapping). Two decisions were LOCKED at create-story: (1) gate name `scripts/microcopy/`; (2) v1 footprint = forward-compat + a bounded allow-listed `apps/admin` scan (green-with-teeth).

**Inherited item Closed (Story 1.17):**

- **D4-1.11b `packages/tokens` leg** — **Closed by [edit].** Story 1.17 IS the extraction trigger (the design-system foundation lands once — not a 2nd admin surface, which the DD-1 deferral named). The inline `apps/admin/src/styles.css` `@theme` status palette is subsumed into `@twt/tokens` (same names, same §4.10 AA-contrast values — no visual/contrast regression; integrity-page tests stay green; the compiled admin CSS bundle still carries the exact pairs), and admin now `@import`s the generated `@twt/tokens/theme.css`. The original D4-1.11b entry is annotated inline. See ADR-0016.

**Closed by [edit] (Story 1.17):**

- **Story 1.17 binding deliverables** — **Closed by [edit].** Landed: (a) the `@twt/tokens` token registry (`src/tokens.ts` — semantic color aliases per §8 + general aliases + the firm admin status palette; the five UX-DR9 type-role faces + Latin pairings; the four named spacing tokens; the four border tokens, no shadow per FM-3; FM-14 #1 semantic-name invariant + #3 per-group comments; unit-tested in `tests/tokens.test.ts`); (e) the pure `renderThemeCss()` semantic CSS generator (`src/theme.ts`) → the committed `src/theme.css` + the FM-4 determinism gate (`scripts/check-theme-determinism.ts`, the `tokens:check-theme-determinism` turbo task, the `tokens-theme-check` ci.yml job); (b)+(c)+(d) the `scripts/microcopy/` gate (pure `lib.ts` + impure `check.ts` + 34 fixture tests + README + the root `microcopy.yaml` config + root `microcopy:check`/`microcopy:test` + the `microcopy` ci.yml job) enforcing FM-14 #2 magic-number colors + the vocabulary register + tone prohibitions + the amendment-A2 numeral discipline, every finding naming file+line+canonical replacement, green-with-teeth over the `apps/admin` slice (teeth proven end-to-end). ADR-0016 authored (drafted). See ADR-0016.

**Resolved via explicit deferral (Story 1.17):**

- **`packages/ui` extraction (D4-1.11b second leg)** — **Resolved via explicit deferral.** `packages/ui` is reserved for TWT-data-shape **composed components** under the second-consumer promotion rule (architecture §Component-layer-boundary L4459-4463), which is unmet today — no composed component has a second consumer. It stays a stub. **Re-trigger:** a second consumer needs the same composed component (e.g. Sahyog List / Yogdaan Bahi reused across surfaces in Epic 2+).
- **`packages/i18n` numeral utilities (`toHindiNumeral` / `toGregorianNumeral` / `formatCurrency`)** — **Resolved via explicit deferral.** Epics L1401 is explicit: the i18n utility ships at **Story 2.1, not Epic 1**; `packages/i18n` is a stub. Story 1.17 ships the numeral **discipline** (the rules + the lint), not the runtime conversion functions. The `microcopy` gate's "inline numeral formatting outside the utility" check flags-by-policy now and tightens once 2.1 lands the utility. **Re-trigger:** Story 2.1 lands `packages/i18n`. — **UPDATE (Story 2.1, 2026-06-20): Closed by [edit].** The re-trigger fired and is fulfilled: `toHindiNumeral`/`toGregorianNumeral`/`formatCurrency` landed in `packages/i18n` (`src/number.ts`, `src/currency.ts`) with the amendment-A2 contract encoded; the microcopy gate's numeral note is now anchored to `packages/i18n` as the sanctioned home. See the Story 2.1 section above + ADR-0018.
- **FM-14 magic-number check — spacing / border / font px-literal facet** — **Resolved via explicit deferral.** The v1 magic-number check is **color-only** (color is the token facet with committed values). Demanding replacement of px/rem literals with spacing/border tokens whose values are **placeholder until P0-2** would be incoherent. **Re-trigger:** P0-2 commits real px values for the spacing/border tokens — then extend the magic-number check to flag px/rem length literals in component code.
- **Broad member-surface vocabulary + numeral enforcement** — **Resolved via explicit deferral.** At v1 the member-address terms (`user`/`customer`/`donor`/`Late Teacher`) scan only `scope.copy_globs` (empty until member surfaces land) and the numeral checks find nothing on the admin slice (no Devanagari / inline formatting). Teeth grow surface-by-surface, data-driven, with no gate code change. **Re-trigger:** Epic 2+ member surfaces populate `scope.copy_globs` (locale strings / templates).
- **Style Dictionary migration** — **Resolved via explicit deferral.** v1 is a hand-rolled TS module by decision; adding Style Dictionary before a consumer needs it is the "scaffolding with no consumer" trap (OQ-UX-14). **Re-trigger:** second-Pariwar provisioning OR the first non-TS consumer.
- **FM-2 per-device fallback ladder + FM-5 Devanagari-aware contrast + type-scale sizes** — **Resolved via explicit deferral.** `@twt/tokens` commits the role→face mapping + a documented substitution policy + AA-contrast discipline; the empirical per-device fallback ladder, the firm contrast values, and the exact type-scale sizes are **P0-2 prototype device-validation outputs**. **Re-trigger:** the P0-2 prototype.

**Cross-reference (NOT built here):**

- **D4-1.9: admin web UI (post-1.17)** — the `apps/admin` login UI + admin chrome land **with the design-system foundation, post-Story 1.17** (deferred-work D4-1.9). Story 1.17 is the gating dependency: the token API is kept clean + documented (`packages/tokens/README.md`) for that immediate next consumer. Cross-reference only — not built here.

## Story 1.16d — resolutions + new deferrals (governance author-commit, 2026-06-16)

Closure-language posture per [[feedback_closure_language_precision]]: **Closed by [edit]** only where this story produced the artifact; everything else is **Resolved via explicit deferral** (gap intentional, rationale + trigger recorded). The binding AC of Story 1.16d is the **FR-100 / FR-7 Hook 1 enum-tag gate** — a repo-global verification engine + the `benefit-mechanism` CI job asserting every Niyamavali rule record carries `benefit_mechanism ∈ {pool, reserve}` and that v1 rules only ever use `pool` (architecture §1.13 Hook 1 L1133-1147). It is the SIBLING of 1.16c's Hook 2 (non-add): Hook 2 asserts ZERO forbidden artifacts exist; Hook 1 (this story) asserts EVERY rule record is tagged. It is an INVARIANT SCAN of current state, **NOT a git-diff** (no `fetch-depth: 0` — mirror `schema-diff`/`pii-scrape`, not `friction-budget`). **NO-OP UNTIL EPIC 2** (the rule registry — `clause_versions` + seed + `apps/api/src/modules/rules/` — is Story 2.3): at v1, check (a) rule-record tag scan + check (c) rule-table schema-column find nothing, while check (b) enum-definition cross-check has **teeth now** (the shipped `BenefitMechanism` z.enum equals `benefit-mechanism.yaml` `mechanisms`), so the gate is green-by-construction-**with-teeth** on the introducing PR. 1.16d **installs** the continuous gate; Story 14.5 records the final-state verification + flips `v1_only` at v2.

**No inherited item to Close:** a grep of this file for `1.16d` / `benefit_mechanism` / FR-7 enum-tag routing returns nothing — unlike 1.16c (which Closed D9-1.2), Story 1.16d inherits **no** binding deferred-work entry and **no** adjacent cluster. This story only *adds* new deferrals.

**Closed by [edit] (Story 1.16d):**

- **Story 1.16d binding gate + engine + enum + config** — **Closed by [edit].** Landed: `benefit-mechanism.yaml` (repo-root versioned config — `version: 1` + `mechanisms: [pool, reserve]` + `v1_only: true` [realizes the `BENEFIT_MECHANISM_V1_ONLY` build flag as a versioned/reviewable field] + `v1_permitted: [pool]` + `rule_sources` [`tables: [clause_versions]` set from day one; `seed_globs`/`fixture_globs` empty at v1] + FR-100/FR-7 header + v2-flip governance note) → `parseBenefitMechanismConfig` throws-on-malformed (mirror `parseFr100Config`); the forward-compat `BenefitMechanism` z.enum at `packages/contracts/src/rules/benefit-mechanism.ts` (barrel-exported; `openapi/v1.yaml` byte-identical — the 1.16b precedent); the pure + importable engine in `scripts/benefit-mechanism/lib.ts` (the `RuleRecord` seam + `validateRuleRecord`/`validateRuleRecords` [check a] + `enumMatchesMechanisms`/`checkEnumDefinition` [check b, dynamic-import] + `scanRuleTableColumns` [check c] + the structured `extractFromSqlInserts`/`extractFromJsonSeed` extractors + `formatFinding`); the impure `scripts/benefit-mechanism/check.ts`; and the repo-global `benefit-mechanism` CI gate (root `benefit:check`/`benefit:test` + the `benefit-mechanism` ci.yml job mirroring `schema-diff`, NO `fetch-depth: 0`). Fixture-unit-tested (33 tests: check (a) untagged/invalid-enum/`reserve`-while-`v1_only`/non-string → finding-naming-the-rule + `pool`/empty-set → clean + v2-flag-flipped `reserve` → clean; check (b) `enumMatchesMechanisms` order-insensitive match/mismatch + `checkEnumDefinition` against the real shipped enum [match → clean, drifted config → finding]; check (c) rule-table without column → finding, with column / absent / null → clean; `parseBenefitMechanismConfig` valid → typed + malformed [version/mechanisms/v1_only/v1_permitted-not-in-mechanisms] → throw; both extractors). See ADR-0015.

**Resolved via explicit deferral (Story 1.16d):**

- **TS / object-literal rule-record seed extraction** — **Resolved via explicit deferral.** The v1 gate ships the **structured** extractors — SQL `INSERT` rows (`extractFromSqlInserts`) + JSON seed records (`extractFromJsonSeed`) + the snapshot column check — all fixture-tested. A **TS-object-literal seed extractor** (e.g. a `seed.ts` exporting an array of rule literals) is speculative before Epic 2's concrete seed shape exists, so it is deferred. **Re-trigger:** Story 2.3 lands the `clause_versions` seed shape — Epic 2's seed-loader calls the importable `validateRuleRecords()` and populates `rule_sources.seed_globs`/`fixture_globs` (at which point check (a) acquires teeth with no gate code change; a TS-literal extractor is added only if the seed lands as `.ts` rather than `.sql`/`.json`).
- **`pools.benefit_mechanism` denormalized-tag cross-check** — **Resolved via explicit deferral.** The pool schema also carries `benefit_mechanism` "per Story 1.16d CI" (epics L2622), but the pool table is **Story 8.x** and absent in v1 — there is nothing to cross-check. The gate's `rule_sources.tables` is `[clause_versions]` only. **Re-trigger:** the pool registry lands at Epic 8 — extend `rule_sources.tables` (or add a denormalized-tag cross-check leg) to assert the pool snapshot tag matches its rule's mechanism.

**Story 14.5 hand-off (recorded, NOT built here):**

- **Story 14.5 — FR-100 `benefit_mechanism` Tag Verification (Continuous CI Gate Final Closure)** (epics L4314-4329) — 1.16d **installs** the gate; it runs continuously on every PR across Epic 2 + downstream consumers. Story 14.5 records the **final verification** (gate ran across Epic 2+, every v1 rule fixture/migration/seed carries `benefit_mechanism: 'pool'`, `reserve` tags zero v1 rules, `BENEFIT_MECHANISM_V1_ONLY` set) and then **flips `v1_only: false`** at v2 / Durghatana Sahayata activation (new `reserve` rules are greenfield Niyamavali additions per Story 2.3; v1 `pool` rules stay replay-identical) — the flip = a trustee-attested ADR per Story 14.7 (capability-bar update). The 14.5↔1.16d pairing mirrors 14.4↔1.16c. Do NOT build 14.5's verification artifact here — cross-reference only.

**Epic 1 governance CI-gate cluster — complete (informational):**

- With Story 1.16d, the **Epic 1 governance CI-gate cluster is complete**: 1.16a (friction-budget · UX-DR3) · 1.16b (pii-scrape · FR-74) · 1.16c (schema-diff · FR-100 Hook 2) · 1.16d (benefit-mechanism · FR-100 Hook 1) all `done`. Informational only — sprint-status / the Epic 1 retrospective own any epic-closure action, not this story.

## Deferred from: code review of 1-16d-benefit-mechanism-tag-ci-gate.md (2026-06-16)

Review layers: Blind Hunter ✓ · Edge Case Hunter ✓ · Acceptance Auditor ✓. 1 decision-needed · 10 patches written to story; 14 dismissed; 10 deferred items below.

- **CI job no timeout** — consistent with all sibling gates (1.16a/b/c share the same gap); a hung `pnpm install` could block the merge queue for up to 6 hours. Re-trigger: add `timeout-minutes` to all 1.16x CI jobs in a single governance-infra PR.
- **CI `setup-node` missing `cache-dependency-path: pnpm-lock.yaml`** — cache key may not include lockfile hash; stale cache served after lockfile changes. Consistent with sibling gates. Re-trigger: fix across all 1.16x jobs together.
- **`resolveGlobs` symlink walk has no cycle detection or depth limit** — `entry.isDirectory()` returns `true` for symlinked directories; a symlink loop in the repo would cause infinite recursion. Extremely unlikely but unguarded. Re-trigger: if symlinked directories are introduced to the monorepo.
- **`collectMigrationSql` scans ALL migration files on every CI run** — no pre-filter by table relevance; post-Epic-2 with hundreds of migrations this becomes an O(N) scan. Also: a drop-and-recreate migration could produce false-positive/negative if column positions shift. Re-trigger: Epic 2 lands the `clause_versions` migration; filter `collectMigrationSql` to only files that mention a configured table name.
- **`v1_permitted` field becomes dead code when `v1_only` flips to `false` at Story 14.5** — no log output difference between the v1Only=true and v1Only=false enforcement paths; the config field is still parsed and validated but silently unenforced after the flip. Re-trigger: Story 14.5 (`v1_only: false` flip) — add a log line when `v1Only` is false indicating the v1-only constraint is not active.
- **`extractFromJsonSeed` may extract sibling non-rule objects from multi-type seed files** — the `_config` parameter is unused; any object in a declared seed file with an ID-like field is validated as a rule record, potentially producing false positives from metadata/tags arrays. Intentionally deferred (seed shape unknown before Story 2.3). Re-trigger: Story 2.3 lands `clause_versions` seed shape — scope the extractor to the declared table's records.
- **`checkEnumDefinition` dynamic import path not anchored to `import.meta.url`** — hardcoded `'../../packages/contracts/src/rules/benefit-mechanism.ts'` resolves relative to `lib.ts` under tsx, which is correct, but is latently fragile if invocation context or module resolution changes. Tests currently pass. Re-trigger: any bundling, compilation, or test-runner change that alters tsx module resolution.
- **`loadLatestSnapshot` returns `null` with no explicit warning** — when the drizzle meta directory exists but contains no matching `*_snapshot.json` files, check (c) silently no-ops with only a `(none)` in the snapshot log line; no clear "check (c) disabled" signal. Re-trigger: if snapshot naming conventions change or a developer accidentally clears the meta directory.
- **SQL `NULL` literal value produces misleading finding message** — `INSERT INTO clause_versions (id, benefit_mechanism) VALUES ('R5', NULL)` yields a finding reporting `benefit_mechanism='NULL'` as "not one of pool|reserve" rather than the more accurate "missing required tag." Re-trigger: Epic 2 seeds rules and NULL is encountered in a migration.
- **Test module-level `parseBenefitMechanismConfig` at module scope** — `SAMPLE_YAML` parse is called at module initialization in `lib.test.ts`; a throw here crashes the entire test file, masking which individual test cases failed. Re-trigger: if `SAMPLE_YAML` is ever edited incorrectly.

## Deferred from: code review of 1-16c-schema-diff-ci-gate.md (2026-06-16)

Review layers: Blind Hunter ✓ · Edge Case Hunter ✓ · Acceptance Auditor ✗ (timed out — AC compliance not confirmed). 2 patches written to story; 5 dismissed; 8 deferred items below.

- **CR-1.16c-D1: `scanEndpoints` scans all string literals, not just route-registration call sites** — error messages, log strings, or test fixtures in `apps/api/src/**/*.ts` that mention `/payout-destinations` could trigger false positives; v1 risk is zero (gate is green by construction); concern is about future false positives in `apps/api/src/` as the codebase grows. **Re-trigger:** if `pnpm schema:check` produces a false-positive finding from a non-route string (error message, fixture, log line). [`scripts/schema-diff/lib.ts:scanEndpoints`]
- **CR-1.16c-D2: `scanZodSchemas` misses `export default` and re-export forms** — `export default class PayoutDestinationService {}`, `export { PayoutDestinationSchema } from "./schema"`, and `export * as PayoutDestinationAPI from "./api"` are not matched by the current export regex; underlying named `export const` definitions ARE caught at their source file (so the primary concern is barrel-file re-exports adding a new alias with a forbidden name). **Re-trigger:** if a barrel index file in `packages/contracts/src/` re-exports a forbidden identifier under a new `PayoutDestination*` name via `export { X as PayoutDestinationY }`. [`scripts/schema-diff/lib.ts:scanZodSchemas`]
- **CR-1.16c-D3: `scanTables` SQL scan misses multiline `CREATE TABLE` DDL** — the line-by-line regex requires the `CREATE TABLE` keyword and the table name to appear on the same line; drizzle-kit always generates single-line DDL so this only affects hand-written migrations; the snapshot is the authoritative primary source (belt-and-suspenders SQL scan would miss multiline hand-written SQL). **Re-trigger:** if a hand-written migration with multiline DDL is introduced (drizzle-kit-generated SQL is always single-line). [`scripts/schema-diff/lib.ts:scanTables`]
- **CR-1.16c-D4: Template-literal route paths not allowlistable by runtime value** — `scanEndpoints` captures `` `/api/${version}/payout-destinations` `` as-is (including `${version}` interpolation), so a v2 allow entry keyed by the actual runtime path (e.g. `/api/v1/payout-destinations`) would not suppress it; the finding artifact would be `` /api/${version}/payout-destinations ``. **Re-trigger:** if v2 adds a route to the payout surface via a template literal and tries to allowlist it. [`scripts/schema-diff/lib.ts:scanEndpoints`]
- **CR-1.16c-D5: Column allow-list key format mismatch (bare name vs table.column)** — `isAllowed('column', colName, config)` checks the bare column name (`payout_destination_id`), but the finding artifact records `table.column` (`members.payout_destination_id`); an allow entry written as `{ kind: column, artifact: members.payout_destination_id }` would silently not match; the correct allow key is the bare column name. Should be documented in `scripts/schema-diff/README.md` at v2 allow-entry authoring time. **Re-trigger:** v2 adds a payout-destination column and tries to allowlist it. [`scripts/schema-diff/lib.ts:scanColumns`]
- **CR-1.16c-D6: `tsx` version not pinned in root `package.json`** — `pnpm schema:check` invokes `tsx` directly with no explicit version constraint in the script; if `tsx` is a transitive devDependency at an unpinned range, a major-version upgrade could silently break the gate. Pre-existing pattern (shared with `friction:check`). **Re-trigger:** `pnpm schema:check` fails unexpectedly after a `pnpm update`. [`package.json`]
- **CR-1.16c-D7: CI `cache: pnpm` key could miss warm cache on runner divergence** — `actions/setup-node` with `cache: pnpm` uses the default store key; if runner OS images diverge between the `install` job and the `schema-diff` job, the warm cache is missed and packages re-download. Pre-existing pattern (consistent with `pii-scrape`/`friction-budget` sibling jobs). **Re-trigger:** intermittent slow `schema-diff` job runs. [`.github/workflows/ci.yml:schema-diff`]
- **CR-1.16c-D8: `collectTsFiles` picks up `*.test.ts` files under scan roots** — the recursive walker excludes only `node_modules` and dotfiles; test files under `apps/api/src/` or `packages/contracts/src/` that reference forbidden paths in fixture assertions could produce false positives; not a current issue (gate is green by construction; no test files reference the forbidden paths today). **Re-trigger:** if a test file in a scanned root uses the forbidden endpoint string as an expected-to-fail fixture value and `pnpm schema:check` fails. [`scripts/schema-diff/check.ts:collectTsFiles`]

## Story 1.16c — resolutions + new deferrals (governance author-commit, 2026-06-16)

Closure-language posture per [[feedback_closure_language_precision]]: **Closed by [edit]** only where this story produced the artifact; everything else is **Resolved via explicit deferral** (gap intentional, rationale + trigger recorded). The binding AC of Story 1.16c is the **FR-100 schema-diff non-add gate** — a repo-global forbidden-pattern scan (`fr-100-non-add.yaml` registry + four precision-scoped scanners + the `schema-diff` CI job) asserting the v1 codebase ships ZERO payout-destination surface (no `payout_destinations` table / no `payout_destination*` column / no `/payout-destinations*` endpoint / no `*PayoutDestination*` Zod schema). It is an INVARIANT SCAN of current state, **NOT a git-diff** (no `fetch-depth: 0` — mirror `pii-scrape`, not `friction-budget`); it is green by construction on the introducing PR (the four real scan roots are clean; the dozens of documentary keyword mentions across `_bmad-output`/`docs`/`*.md`/`sprint-status.yaml`/the gate's own fixtures + config are precision-scoped OUT). 1.16c **installs** the continuous gate; Story 14.4 records the final-state verification.

**Closed by [edit] (Story 1.16c):**

- **D9-1.2: Substantive Story 1.16c v1-baseline forbidden-pattern asserts** — **Closed by [edit].** The four scanners (`scanTables`/`scanColumns` over the latest drizzle `migrations/meta/*_snapshot.json` + `migrations/*.sql`; `scanEndpoints` over `apps/api/src/**/*.ts` route literals; `scanZodSchemas` over `packages/contracts/src/**/*.ts` exports) + the `fr-100-non-add.yaml` registry (loud-throw `parseFr100Config`, mirroring `parseFrictionBudgetYaml`) + the repo-global `schema-diff` CI gate (`scripts/schema-diff/{check.ts,lib.ts,lib.test.ts,README.md}` + root `schema:check`/`schema:test`) all landed + fixture-unit-tested (22 tests: each scanner positive→finding-with-named-pointer + negative `member_addresses`/`mailing_address_id`/`/api/v1/members`/`MemberAddressSchema`→clean, allow-list→clean, malformed-config→throw). The **architecture-committed disjoint ownership** is honored: the lightweight `db:check` drift primitive (Story 1.2, `drizzle-kit check`) stays as-is; the substantive FR-100 asserts are the NEW repo-global `schema-diff` gate, NOT folded into `drizzle-kit check` (a canned command that cannot host custom asserts). The stale `db-check` ci.yml comment naming 1.16c is reconciled to cross-reference the new job. See ADR-0014.

**Resolved via explicit deferral (Story 1.16c):**

- **W11 (un-generated-drift leg): `db:check` does not catch un-generated schema drift** — the "substantive schema-diff CI" half is now **Closed by [edit]** (this story — see D9-1.2). The *separate* limitation that `drizzle-kit check` does not catch **un-generated** drift (a developer editing `src/schema/*.ts` without running `db:generate`) is NOT in this story's FR-100 scope and is **Resolved via explicit deferral**. **Re-trigger:** a focused `db:generate` determinism / no-pending-drift CI assert (its own effort). Note: the FR-100 gate reading the *committed* snapshot is unaffected by un-generated drift — it asserts over committed artifacts.
- **D1-1.4: OpenAPI breaking-change semantic-diff CI gate** — **Resolved via explicit deferral.** A *different* gate (a semantic-diff of `openapi/v1.yaml` for breaking changes, architecture §3.2 L1856-1860) loosely co-routed to "1.16c" in deferred-work; it is orthogonal to FR-100 non-add and out of this story's scope. The route surface is still thin (admin/auth only). **Re-trigger:** the route surface substantively populates at Epic 2/3/11+.
- **W10-CR1.6 + W12-CR1.6: `@twt/domain` exports-map pair** — **Resolved via explicit deferral** (BigDev decision at 1.16c dev: re-defer both; Dev Notes → Decision 2). The two warnings (`"./policies"` `exports` registration + the `@twt/events`→`@twt/domain` `src/test-utils/integration-setup.js` deep relative import a future exports map would break) are a **coupled packaging concern orthogonal to the FR-100 gate**: adding a *partial* exports map (just `"./policies"`) without a `"./src/test-utils/*"` wildcard would itself *cause* W12's break, so the two must land together. Keeping this governance gate single-purpose, both defer. **Re-trigger:** a dedicated `@twt/domain` exports-map hardening pass that designs the full map at once (subpath registrations + the `src/test-utils/*` wildcard or a top-level re-export) + updates the `@twt/events` import.

**Story 14.4 hand-off (recorded, NOT built here):**

- **Story 14.4 — FR-100 Schema-Diff Verification (Continuous CI Gate Final Closure)** (epics L4294-4312) — 1.16c **installs** the gate; it runs continuously on every PR across Epics 1-13. Story 14.4 records the **final verification** in `_bmad-output/research/fr-100-schema-diff-verification.md` (CI-run history + final-state proof) and confirms the gate "continues to run post-v1 launch as a permanent governance gate" (v2 allowlist extension requires a trustee-attested ADR + capability-bar update per Story 14.7). Do NOT build 14.4's verification artifact here — cross-reference only.

## Story 1.16a deferred + discharged (governance author-commit, 2026-06-16)

Closure-language posture per [[feedback_closure_language_precision]]: **Closed by [edit]** only where this story produced the artifact; everything else is **Resolved via explicit deferral** (gap intentional, rationale + trigger recorded) or **Out of scope / re-deferred**. The binding AC of Story 1.16a is the **friction-budget gate** (both facets — `friction-budget.yaml` metric ceilings + `friction-budget.md` named-payer ledger, `scripts/friction-budget/check.ts`, the `friction-budget` CI job). The adjacent governance-lint cluster (AC-7) was assessed item-by-item and rationally drained, not silently inherited whole.

**Closed by [edit] (Story 1.16a):**

- **D1-1.6: pg.Pool / service-role connection import-rule lint** — **Closed by [edit]** as the achievable, lint-green realization of architecture §1.2 (lines 736-740, 764-770). Landed `@typescript-eslint/no-restricted-imports` on the `pg` module (`allowTypeImports: true`) in `packages/eslint-config-twt/index.js`, with a carve-out override for the data layer + tests, plus positive/negative coverage in `packages/eslint-config-twt/index.test.js`. **Scope deviation recorded (ADR-0012 + completion notes):** the architectural property names `cross-tenant/` as the sole carve-out, but `cross-tenant/run-as-cross-tenant.ts` only *receives* pools by parameter (`import type pg`) — it constructs none; the actual `new pg.Pool()` sites are `packages/domain/src/db.ts` (THE app pool) + `test-utils` + tests. A blanket `pg`-ban with a `cross-tenant/`-only carve-out would red-fail `lint` across 28 legit `pg` imports (10 real Pool sites). So the carve-out is role-based (the shared flat config runs per-package — `eslint .` from each workspace dir — so `files` globs match package-relative and a package-path glob like `packages/domain/**` can't match): the conventional DB-module file (`**/db.ts`) + `**/test-utils/**` + test files. The rule bans **value** imports of `pg` outside those roles (apps + other packages must receive connections via DI), allowing `import type`. Green today (no app-src constructs a pool); guards future rogue value-imports. The narrower service-role-specific rule re-triggers with **D9-1.6** (when a distinct `twt_service` BYPASSRLS pool becomes its own construction site).

**Resolved via explicit deferral (Story 1.16a):**

- **AC-6: live critical-render-path TIMING on the canonical device (3GB Android, architecture line 34)** — **Resolved via explicit deferral.** No device-farm / CPU-throttled-Lighthouse harness exists in CI; building one now would invent infrastructure the epic plan doesn't schedule here. The statically measurable proxies (JS bundle bytes + page-weight per route) ship now in `friction-budget.yaml` (the `critical_render_path_ms` entry is a marked placeholder under `deferred_metrics`). **Re-trigger:** a throttled-Lighthouse-CI harness lands alongside the Story 2.5 Astro shell or the Story 1.17 design-system surface. (`friction-budget.yaml`, `scripts/friction-budget/`)
- **D5-1.4: type-shadowing-of-`packages/contracts/`-exports ESLint rule** — **Resolved via explicit deferral.** No off-the-shelf `no-restricted-*` config expresses "a `type`/`interface` redefining a name exported by `packages/contracts/`"; it needs a bespoke cross-file-aware AST rule — outside the "cheap + self-contained" bar AC-7 sets for co-landing. Story 1.4's structural mitigation (contracts as source-of-truth + per-domain README reminders) stands; the `TODO` row remains in `eslint-config-twt` §2. **Re-trigger:** a custom `eslint-plugin-twt` AST-rule effort (e.g. the quarterly lint-review cadence or a focused governance story). (`packages/eslint-config-twt/index.js`)
- **D6-1.4: `.strict()`-default-on-Zod-objects ESLint rule** — **Resolved via explicit deferral.** Needs a bespoke AST rule (flag `z.object(...)` not chained with `.strict()`); no off-the-shelf rule. Story 1.4's runtime helper `_common/strict.ts` + per-domain reminders are the structural mitigation. **Re-trigger:** the custom `eslint-plugin-twt` AST-rule effort. (`packages/eslint-config-twt/index.js`)
- **D7-1.4: validator-presence-per-route ESLint rule** — **Resolved via explicit deferral.** Now *unblocked* (apps/api routes substantive since 1.9), but the rule itself is a bespoke AST rule (assert every route in `apps/api/modules/` declares a Zod validator) tied to the route-registration shape — not an off-the-shelf config. **Re-trigger:** the custom `eslint-plugin-twt` AST-rule effort. (`packages/eslint-config-twt/index.js`, `apps/api/src/modules/`)
- **D12-1.4: `*Id`-must-be-branded ESLint enforcer** — **Resolved via explicit deferral.** The branded ID *types* closed at Story 1.7 (`packages/domain/src/ids/`); only the enforcer lint rule is outstanding, and it too needs a bespoke AST rule. **Re-trigger:** the custom `eslint-plugin-twt` AST-rule effort. (`packages/eslint-config-twt/index.js`)
- **D4-1.4: per-tag bundle-subsetting CI test** — **Resolved via explicit deferral; friction-budget-adjacent.** Architecture §4.11.1 (lines 2793-2794): "CI test asserts no admin-only operations land in the mobile bundle; no member-only operations in the admin bundle." No member/admin JS bundle exists in `pnpm turbo run build` yet (same no-op rationale as the friction-budget metric facet, AC-2). **Re-trigger:** member/admin bundles land (Epic 2 public Astro / later member web/native) — the same surfaces the friction-budget metric facet begins measuring. (`friction-budget.yaml`, future bundle CI)

**Out of scope / re-deferred (documentary-only governance, loosely tagged "1.16a friction territory" — NOT in the AC-7 lint cluster):**

- **Escrow-attestation CI** (`docs/escrow/code-escrow/README.md` — sole-Solo-Builder-admin "MUST NOT" is documentary only) — re-deferred. CI enforcement is a separate governance-tooling effort.

(`infra/dokploy/`, `infra/gcp/`, `apps/api` origin guard)
- **D3-1.13: FR-88 member-facing surface widgets (signup, claim filing, helpdesk)** — Story 1.13 ships the consume-everywhere primitive (`@twt/edge` verifier + the documented widget-mount recipe in apps/admin LoginPage) but `apps/public` is a stub (the Astro SSR shell is Story 2.5, extended in Epic 11a). The member-facing FR-88 forms are not built. **Re-trigger:** Story 2.5 / Epic 11a — mount the Turnstile widget (member site key) on the real signup / claim-filing / helpdesk forms and wire their server endpoints through `@twt/edge`. (`apps/public`, `packages/edge`)
- **D4-1.13: admin password-reset UI Turnstile widget** — the `passwordResetRequest` server endpoint IS Turnstile-gated (AC-3) + accepts `turnstileToken`, but the admin password-reset UI is deferred (DD-2 from Story 1.11b) — no client function exists yet, so no widget is mounted on a reset form. **Re-trigger:** the admin password-reset UI lands (post-1.17) — add an `api.requestPasswordReset(...)` client fn that threads a Turnstile token + mount the widget. (`apps/admin/src/api/client.ts`, `apps/admin/src/routes`)
- **D5-1.13: Bot Management plan-tier enablement** — `var.enable_bot_management` defaults false; the `cf.bot_management.score`-based WAF rules require a Cloudflare plan that populates the score (Enterprise Bot Management, or Pro/Business Super Bot Fight Mode). The architecture commits the capability, not the SKU (ADR-0010). **Re-trigger:** the trust purchases a qualifying Cloudflare plan — set `enable_bot_management = true` and verify the score-based rules engage. (`infra/cloudflare/waf.tf`)

## Deferred from: code review of 1-13-cloudflare-bot-management-turnstile-edge-protection (2026-06-15)

- **CR-D1-1.13: Stale spent Turnstile token on hypothetical MFA-stage back-navigation** — In `LoginPage.tsx`, `turnstileToken` state is cleared on submit failure but not on the happy-path stage transition to `mfa`. If a future UI flow allows back-navigation from the MFA stage to credentials, the stale (already-spent) token would be submitted and return `timeout-or-duplicate` → generic 401 with no visible indication the widget needs re-solving. Pre-existing design: back-navigation is not currently exposed. Re-trigger: any future admin SPA flow that routes back from MFA to the credentials form. (`apps/admin/src/routes/LoginPage.tsx`)
- **CR-D2-1.13: `starts_with` sensitive-path matching inherits strict bot threshold for future prefix-sharing routes** — The `sensitive_paths_expression` in `locals.tf` uses `starts_with(http.request.uri.path, "/api/v1/auth/login")`, which also matches `/api/v1/auth/login-success` or any future sub-path with the same prefix. New routes added under a covered prefix silently inherit the stricter bot-score challenge threshold. Pre-existing design choice in deferred-apply HCL; all current paths are correct. Re-trigger: whenever a new route is added under a sensitive prefix — verify whether it should inherit or be excluded. (`infra/cloudflare/locals.tf`)

## Deferred from: code review of 1-12-pg-boss-job-queue-idempotency-keyed-store (2026-06-15)

- **CR-D1-1.12: `hashtext()` 32-bit advisory lock collision** — `pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext($1))` collapses the key space to 32 bits; two distinct keys that collide cause unnecessary false serialization. Data integrity is preserved by `ON CONFLICT (key)`. The spec mandates `hashtext()` (no extra hashing util). Re-trigger: post-ship hardening pass or a high-throughput consumer hits measurable false-lock contention. (`packages/domain/src/idempotency/keyed-store.ts`)
- **CR-D2-1.12: `apps/jobs` Dockerfile runtime stage incomplete** — the runtime stage copies only `apps/jobs/dist` and `package.json`, no `node_modules` or workspace package dists. `node dist/src/boot.js` would fail `ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND` in a real container run. Pre-existing pattern across all app Dockerfiles (api, admin, public also incomplete); Docker runtime completeness is a separate workstream. Re-trigger: first production deploy preparation for the jobs image. (`apps/jobs/Dockerfile`)
- **CR-D3-1.12: Health server has no request/headers timeout** — `http.createServer` with no `headersTimeout`/`requestTimeout` leaves sockets open indefinitely on slow-read. Internal port only; hardening concern. Re-trigger: security hardening pass or a load-balancer misconfiguration causes socket accumulation. (`apps/jobs/src/boot.ts`)
- **CR-D4-1.12: `status` column has no CHECK DB constraint** — `status text NOT NULL` accepts arbitrary strings; only the TypeScript type enforces `'pending' | 'completed'`. Drizzle ORM limitation (no `CHECK` emitted for text columns by default). Re-trigger: a future status enum change or a raw-INSERT path bypasses the app layer. (`packages/domain/migrations/0012_idempotency-keys.sql`)

**Newly opened (Resolved via explicit deferral):**

Rejected for v1 because worker execution context does not `SET LOCAL app.pariwar_id` and the store is cross-cutting infra, not tenant domain data. **Re-trigger:** a future consumer that needs STRICT per-tenant key isolation enforced at the DB row level (add `pariwar_id` + composite PK + scoped RLS, mirroring `role_grants`). (`packages/domain/src/schema/idempotency_keys.ts` + `policies/idempotency-keys-rls.ts`)

(`apps/api`)
- **D3-1.12: prod first-start CREATE grant for the pg-boss connection role** — **WIRED in 1.15; live apply = operator activation leg.** The `cloud-sql` module emits the one-time `GRANT CREATE ON DATABASE <db> TO <role>;` via the `pgboss_create_grant_sql` output (Terraform's `google_sql_user` can't issue a DB-level GRANT, so it is delivered as runnable SQL); the operator apply-sequence (`infra/gcp/README.md` §Story 1.15) pipes it into psql against the new instance on first start. **Re-trigger:** the operator runs the grant during staging/prod bring-up. (`infra/gcp/modules/cloud-sql/outputs.tf`, `docs/runbooks/job-queue-operations.md §1`)
- **D4-1.12: per-class worker pools + dead-letter drain procedure** — the substrate documents job classes A/B/C (§1.4) + dead-letter, but per-class worker counts + the DLQ drain runbook are operations policy (§5.11) and are NOT hard-coded here. **Re-trigger:** the first Class-A/B consumer with an SLA, or a DLQ-accumulation incident. (`docs/runbooks/job-queue-operations.md §2.6`)

**Re-affirmed deferred (DD-4 — pg-boss substrate now exists; crons NOT graduated):** D2-1.10 (6-hourly mirror cron — still needs a durable `audit_mirror_state` watermark + prod creds) and D3-1.11a (nightly integrity cron — still needs a prod-pointed run from a separate execution environment + prod creds). Both re-trigger notes updated in their original sections below.

## Deferred from: code review of 1-11b-trustee-facing-audit-log-integrity-verification-ui — Group B (2026-06-14)

- **CR-B-1: `ticket_ref` has no DB-level minimum-length or non-whitespace CHECK** — 512-char cap and non-empty enforcement live only in the Zod contract layer; a direct BYPASSRLS INSERT can store `''` or `'   '`. Write path is currently API-only and Zod-validated. Re-trigger: DB hardening pass. (`packages/domain/migrations/0011_audit-integrity-acknowledgements.sql`)
- **CR-B-2: `acknowledged_at` accepts past/future timestamps via direct BYPASSRLS INSERT** — no CHECK anchors it to `now()`; a future-dated ack would sort first in `ORDER BY acknowledged_at DESC` and prematurely clear the failure banner. The API endpoint omits the column (uses DB default). Re-trigger: DB hardening pass. (`packages/domain/migrations/0011_audit-integrity-acknowledgements.sql`)
- **CR-B-3: Ack bulk-load query has no LIMIT** — `WHERE check_id = ANY($1) ORDER BY acknowledged_at DESC, acknowledgement_id ASC` fetches every ack ever written for the listed checks; unbounded for a check that is repeatedly acknowledged. Re-trigger: ack count approaches observable performance threshold. (`apps/api/src/modules/audit-log/index.ts`)
- **CR-B-4: `breakpoints:true` journal setting means 0011 DDL may partially commit before the `DO` self-test block runs** — if the self-test raises, the DDL is not rolled back (pre-existing pattern identical to 0007/0009). ## Story 1.11b deferred + discharged (surface author-commit, 2026-06-14)

Closure-language posture per [[feedback_closure_language_precision]]: **Closed by [edit]** only where this story produced the artifact; future-Story / future-environment seams are **Resolved via explicit deferral** (gap intentional, trigger recorded).

**Closed by [edit] (defects fixed under 1.11b):**
- **1.11a `verifyChainWalk` boundary-pair incoherence on head-truncation** — when the break is at the chain head (genesis-anchor failure / first-chunk break before any row verifies), `brokenAt` reported `startSeq = <head>` but `endSeq = null`, which migration 0010's `audit_integrity_checks_boundary_pair_coherent` CHECK rejects → `verifyAuditChain` **threw** instead of persisting the failed verdict, defeating the head-truncation tamper-detection path (and Story 1.11b AC-4's head-truncation banner). Fixed so a no-rows-verified break reports `start = null` too (coherent; the break is still localized by `firstBroken*`). The `AuditIntegrityCheckResult` SHAPE is unchanged. Proven by a fresh-DB repro + a regression assertion in the pure head-truncation test. **Closed by [edit]** (`apps/jobs/src/audit/integrity-check.ts`, `apps/jobs/tests/audit/integrity-check.test.ts`).
- **1.10/1.11a `@twt/jobs` live-DB test-file-parallelism flake** — `integrity-check.test.ts` and `mirror.test.ts` both append to the ONE global chain via `writeAuditEntry`; Vitest's parallel file execution interleaved their seq writes, breaking the chunk-walk tests' consecutive-seq assumption (a load-sensitive failure surfaced once `@twt/api` joined the CI integration run). Fixed with `fileParallelism: false` in the jobs Vitest config. **Closed by [edit]** (`apps/jobs/vitest.config.ts`).
- **Pre-existing `scope-tx.spec.ts` `$2`-parameter-reuse bug** — `VALUES ($1, $2, 'auditor', 'pariwar', $2)` made pg deduce two types for `$2` (uuid vs text) → the whole `@twt/api` integration file errored at setup. It never failed CI only because `@twt/api` was not in the integration-tests filter. Fixed (`$3` + a third param) to unblock adding `@twt/api` to the CI filter so the new 1.11b endpoint tests ride CI (Task 10.3). No production code touched. **Closed by [edit]** (`apps/api/tests/integration/scope-tx.spec.ts`, `.github/workflows/ci.yml`).

**Resolved via explicit deferral (newly opened by 1.11b):**
- **D1-1.11b: real cold-mirror last-good-state pointer (graduation of D1-1.11a)** — AC-4's "cold-mirror pointer" is rendered as the HOT-chain proxy (the last `chainValid=true` check's end boundary), explicitly labelled, with a "cold-mirror cross-verification: deferred (D1-1.11a)" line. **Re-trigger:** the live GCS mirror apply + separate-project reader (D1-1.11a) land. (`apps/admin/src/modules/audit-integrity/derive.ts`, `StatusBanner.tsx`)
- **D2-1.11b: helpdesk/ticketing integration for acknowledge (FR-52)** — v1 captures a free-text external `ticketRef` (recording it IS the "investigation ticket opened" artifact, AC-5). **Re-trigger:** the helpdesk module (`apps/api/modules/helpdesk/`, FR-52) lands. (`apps/api/src/modules/audit-log/index.ts`, `apps/admin/src/modules/audit-integrity/AcknowledgeForm.tsx`)
- **D3-1.11b: full OpenAPI→client codegen + tool-choice ADR (DD-7)** — v1 is a hand-written typed `fetch` layer that parses responses with the `@twt/contracts` Zod schemas (single source of types). This also keeps the inherited **CR-D6-1.10 / CR-D7-1.10** (64-char-hex / `seq` int64 pins for a generated SDK) deferred — the hand-written client parses with Zod (`z.number().int()`), so the int32-default-cap risk does not apply to it. **Re-trigger:** standing up the codegen pipeline + its ADR. (`apps/admin/src/api/client.ts`)
- **D4-1.11b: `packages/ui` + `packages/tokens` extraction (DD-1)** — ~~v1 uses Tailwind v4 + Radix directly in `apps/admin/` (theme tokens in `src/styles.css` `@theme`). **Re-trigger:** a 2nd admin surface needs the same atoms.~~ **`packages/tokens` leg CLOSED by Story 1.17** (see the "Story 1.17 — resolutions" section at the top of this file): Story 1.17 IS the extraction trigger (the design-system foundation lands once, NOT a 2nd admin surface) — the inline `@theme` status palette is subsumed into `@twt/tokens` and admin now consumes the generated `@theme`. The **`packages/ui` leg STAYS DEFERRED** (architecture §Component-layer-boundary L4459-4463: `packages/ui` = TWT-data-shape composed components under the second-consumer promotion rule, unmet here → it stays a stub). **Re-trigger (ui leg only):** a second consumer needs the same composed component. (`apps/admin/`, `packages/tokens/`, `packages/ui/`)

(`apps/admin/src/routes/LoginPage.tsx`)
- **D6-1.11b: endpoint-side `audit.verify` RBAC gate (still D4-1.11a)** — ✅ **DISCHARGED by Story 1.15 (Closed by [edit], via D4-1.11a).** The endpoint-side `audit.verify` gate now exists (`requireGlobalPermission`, AC-1b) — `requireAdminSession` is no longer the only server boundary. The UI nav + route gate stays the ADVISORY UX layer (no client change required, DD-6); the server is now the real boundary it always claimed to be. ## Story 1.11a deferred + discharged (substrate author-commit, 2026-06-14 per Decision 2026-06-14-047)

Closure-language posture per [[feedback_closure_language_precision]]: an item is **Closed by [edit]** only where this story produced the artifact; future-Story / future-environment seams are **Resolved via explicit deferral** (gap intentional, trigger recorded).

**Discharged by Story 1.11a (Closed by [edit]) — the inherited 1.10 → 1.11a landmines:**
- **CR-D2-1.10: chunk-boundary / head-truncation blind spot in per-chunk verification** — the integrity walk (`apps/jobs/src/audit/integrity-check.ts`) closes it at the JOB level WITHOUT changing `verifyChainSegment`: a **genesis anchor** (the lowest-seq row of the whole chain must have `prevAuditHash === null`) + a **cross-chunk stitch** (carry the prior chunk's last `auditHash`; assert `chunk[0].prevAuditHash === carriedPrevHash` before each non-first `verifyChainSegment`). Proven by deterministic pure unit tests (boundary deletion + head-truncation) in `apps/jobs/tests/audit/integrity-check.test.ts`. **Closed by [edit]** (Tasks 5.2/5.3/11.1).
- **CR-D11-1.10: seq gaps are benign** — the walk pages with `seq > cursor` and links rows by `auditHash`, NEVER by seq contiguity; a forged `[1,5,9,100]` chain verifies as valid (gap-tolerance unit test). **Closed by [edit]** (Task 5.4/11.1).
- **CR-D10-1.10: mirror-JSONL `recordedAt` is a string** — the v1 hot-DB reader returns a `Date` (no reconstitution needed); the walk is written SOURCE-AGNOSTIC and the future cold-mirror JSONL reader's `new Date(...)` reconstitution requirement is documented in the integrity-check header. **Closed by [edit]** (the producer-consumer contract is now documented; the JSONL reader itself is deferred — see DD-1 below).

**Newly opened (Resolved via explicit deferral):**
- **D1-1.11a: verify-from-COLD-MIRROR in a separate GCP project** — v1 walks the HOT Postgres chain via the service pool (DD-1); the architecture's ideal (read the cold GCS mirror from a separate-project read SA in its own execution environment, §1.5 L850-853 / §2.10) is a NEW `ChunkReader`, not a rewrite. **Re-trigger:** live GCS mirror apply (D1-1.10) + the §5.6 audit-read stack land. (`apps/jobs/src/audit/integrity-check.ts:ChunkReader`)
- **D2-1.11a: live observability sink + alert-policy wiring** — DD-5 ships provider-fluid `IntegrityObservabilitySink` / `IntegrityAlerter` ports + structured-log fakes; `INTEGRITY_OBSERVABILITY_MODE=live` fails closed. **Re-trigger:** Category 5 (§5.6 Cloud Monitoring / Grafana+Loki + the chain-break alert threshold). (`apps/jobs/src/audit/integrity-observability.ts`)
- **D3-1.11a: prod-pointed nightly run + canonical cron** — `.github/workflows/nightly-integrity.yml` runs the synthetic-tamper gate + a walk against a seeded CI DB (mechanism proof). The prod run against the live chain from a separate execution environment + the canonical pg-boss/Cloud-Scheduler cron of all three triggers are deferred. **Re-trigger:** ~~Story 1.12 (pg-boss job queue; D2-1.10) + prod `SERVICE_DATABASE_URL` + separate-project creds (§2.10)~~. **Re-affirmed deferred (Story 1.12, DD-4):** the **pg-boss substrate now EXISTS (Story 1.12)** — `apps/jobs/src/boot.ts` + `boss.schedule(...)` are live — but this cron was **not graduated** here: it still needs a prod-pointed run from a separate execution environment + prod creds. **Re-trigger (updated):** prod `SERVICE_DATABASE_URL` + separate-project read SA (§2.10); wire as a `boss.schedule(...)` cron in `boot.ts` following the idempotency-vacuum precedent.
- **D4-1.11a: RBAC `audit.verify` gate upgrade for the on-demand endpoint** — ✅ **DISCHARGED by Story 1.15 (Closed by [edit]).** Story 1.15 ships the global-scope preHandler this entry asked for — `requireGlobalPermission(deps, key)` (`apps/api/src/modules/rbac/index.ts`) — and retrofits it onto ALL THREE audit-integrity routes (`POST /audit/verify-integrity`, `GET /audit/integrity-checks`, `POST /audit/integrity-checks/:checkId/acknowledge`): each now gates on `[requireAdminSession(deps), requireGlobalPermission(deps, 'audit.verify')]`. An authenticated admin lacking `audit.verify` at `global` scope gets a real **403** (not a hidden nav entry); the global-route 500 landmine is gone (the gate loads grants from `servicePool`, no `request.scopeTx` needed). Proven by the AC-1b matrix in `apps/api/tests/integration/audit-integrity-ui.spec.ts` (super_admin passes; no-grant admin → 403; pariwar-scoped auditor → 403) + the unit matrix `apps/api/tests/unit/require-global-permission.test.ts`. _(was: gated on `requireAdminSession` ONLY because `requirePermissionHook` needs `request.scopeTx` → 500 on a global route.)_ (`apps/api/src/modules/audit-log/index.ts`, `apps/api/src/modules/rbac/index.ts`)
- **D5-1.11a: optional `predecessorLinkageVerified` field on `ChainVerificationResult`** — NOT taken; the job-level stitch closes CR-D2-1.10 with lower blast radius than a pure-function signature change. **Re-trigger:** a caller that needs the per-segment linkage signal directly from `verifyChainSegment`. (`packages/domain/src/audit/hash-chain.ts`)

**Remain deferred (NOT pulled in):** CR-D3-1.10 (ms-precision ISO contract → ADR-0004) + CR-D6-1.10 (64-char-lowercase-hex format pin → ADR-0004 / 1.11b SDK gen) are load-bearing only for a non-JS verifier or an SDK client; v1 is JS-only, so they are noted and left deferred (no gold-plating). CR-D7-1.10 (`seq` `format: int64` in OpenAPI) → Story 1.11b client SDK gen.

## Deferred from: code review of 1-11a-audit-log-integrity-verification-primitive — Group 3 (2026-06-14)

- **`AuditIntegrityCheckResult` has no cross-field constraint: `rowsVerified > 0 → endSeq IS NOT NULL`** — the implementation of `verifyChainWalk` upholds this invariant (lastGood is always set if any rows verify), but neither the Zod schema nor the DB CHECK constraints enforce it. Re-trigger: if a future audit-client needs contract-level enforcement; a `z.refine` or sixth CHECK constraint in migration 0010 would close it. (`packages/contracts/src/audit/integrity-check.ts`, `packages/domain/migrations/0010_audit-integrity-checks-invariants.sql`)
- **`triggerSource` wire type is `z.string()` not a Zod enum** — intentional: Drizzle `text` column is `string`, which would not extend a `z.union(['cron','on_demand','post_mirror'])` type. Valid values documented in schema comment. Re-trigger: when the contracts layer adds a shared `TriggerSource` type that both the domain schema and the contract can reference. (`packages/contracts/src/audit/integrity-check.ts`)
- **Sink and alerter resolved separately at route registration** — two calls to `resolveIntegrity*FromEnv()` read the same env var independently; combining into a single `resolveIntegrityObservabilityFromEnv() → { sink, alerter }` makes the coupled read explicit. Re-trigger: when adding a live observability adapter (D2-1.11a). (`apps/api/src/modules/audit-log/index.ts:52-53`)

## Deferred from: code review of 1-11a-audit-log-integrity-verification-primitive — Group 2 (2026-06-14)

- **`bigint mode:'number'` precision for seq columns in `audit_integrity_checks` schema** — `startSeq`, `endSeq`, `firstBrokenSeq` use `mode:'number'` (IEEE-754 float, safe to 2^53). Matches the existing `audit_log_entries.seq` pattern. Theoretical at any realistic chain length. Re-trigger: if the codebase migrates seq columns to `mode:'bigint'` globally. (`packages/domain/src/schema/audit_integrity_checks.ts`)
- **`rows_verified` integer overflow at 2^31 rows** — `rows_verified` is `integer` (max ~2.1B). A full-chain walk on a mature chain at scale could overflow. Re-trigger: if `rows_verified` is expected to approach 2B in a single run (would require years of high-frequency writes). (`packages/domain/src/schema/audit_integrity_checks.ts`)
- **No `CHECK` constraint on `trigger_source` vocabulary** — `trigger_source` is free-text `NOT NULL`; valid values are `cron|on_demand|post_mirror`. A CHECK or enum would catch caller bugs. Re-trigger: when `trigger_source` vocabulary is ADR-stable and no new values are expected. (`packages/domain/src/schema/audit_integrity_checks.ts`)

## Deferred from: code review of 1-11a-audit-log-integrity-verification-primitive (2026-06-14)

- **No transactional atomicity between verdict INSERT and sink.publish on `servicePool` path** — `verifyAuditChain` commits the verdict row, then calls `sink.publish`; if publish throws the verdict is committed but observability is lost. Only matters with a live adapter (Category 5). Re-trigger: D2-1.11a (live sink landing). (`apps/jobs/src/audit/integrity-check.ts:285-296`)
- **Point-in-time staleness of `endSeq` on `servicePool` path** — new rows may be appended between the last chunk read and the verdict INSERT, so `endSeq` reflects the chain tail at walk-time, not at persist-time. Inherent to non-transactional walk design; endSeq is "what was verified," not "current tail." Re-trigger: if a future requirement needs the verdict to reflect the atomic chain state at insert time. (`apps/jobs/src/audit/integrity-check.ts:269`)
- **`createInMemoryChunkReader` drops second row if two rows share seq** — test-only helper; IDENTITY seq makes production duplicates impossible. Re-trigger: if in-memory reader is ever used for data with non-unique seq. (`apps/jobs/src/audit/integrity-check.ts:227-232`)

## Deferred from: code review of 1-9-admin-authentication (Groups B+C, 2026-06-12)

Closure-language posture per [[feedback_closure_language_precision]]: code patches are **Closed by [edit]** (artifact produced); architectural/structural gaps are **Resolved via explicit deferral** (gap intentional, re-trigger recorded).

**Group B — API Framework:**

- **CR-B-1: `scope.change` audit flood** — `scope.change` audit event emitted on every scoped request, not just transitions; AC-9 intent is state-change events (§2.5); fix requires explicit scope-state delta detection. Deferred pending spec clarification of "scope-change" semantics. Re-trigger: Story 1.9 scoped-route expansion or Story 1.10 audit-log spec authoring.
- **CR-B-2: `SESSION_SECRET` entropy not validated** — all-spaces 32-char string passes the length check; enforcing true entropy in code requires arbitrary heuristics and has limited security benefit over operational discipline. Deferred, operational concern. Re-trigger: ops hardening / infra ADR.
- **CR-B-3: `AuthorizationDeniedError`/`ApiError` ordering in `errorMappingHandler`** — `ApiError` branch fires first; consequence depends on whether `AuthorizationDeniedError` extends `ApiError`; class hierarchy must be confirmed before a safe patch. Deferred pending hierarchy check. Re-trigger: any story touching error-mapping or `AuthorizationDeniedError` class hierarchy.
- **CR-B-4: `buildEncryptionDeps` reads `KMS_TEST_MODE` from `process.env` directly** — breaks injection discipline; migration to `ApiConfig` injection is invasive (the function is also called with pepper-only from tests). Deferred, refactor scope. Re-trigger: encryption-surface refactor story or Story 1.5 D14-1.5 follow-up.

**Group C — Auth Modules:**

- **CR-C-1: Dummy hash uses weak Argon2id params (`m=8,t=1,p=1`)** — timing oracle distinguishing "no such user" from "wrong password"; a timing-equalized no-user path requires precomputing a production-params dummy hash and threading through `AppDeps`. Deferred, design work needed. Re-trigger: any story hardening the `verifyFirstFactor` user-enumeration surface.
- **CR-C-2: Hostile-trustee-class lockout has no trustee-quorum unlock path (AC-1 gap)** — current lockout is time-based only; quorum-unlock requires a lock-tier enum + a separate ops-authenticated unlock route; quorum infrastructure does not exist in Epic 1. Deferred, needs quorum infrastructure. Re-trigger: ops-security story or trustee-management story post-Epic-1.
- **CR-C-3: Session rotation on role change (AC-3 gap)** — role-grant mutation routes are not in Story 1.9; any story landing role mutations MUST call `session.regenerate()` to satisfy AC-3. Deferred, guard must land with the mutation story. Re-trigger: first story adding a role-mutation route.
- **CR-C-4: `recovery_code.consume` audit missing consumed code hash** — `context: { code_hash }` would improve forensic traceability; current audit line lacks the hash. Deferred, completeness gap. Re-trigger: Story 1.10 audit-log substrate or any audit-enrichment story.
- **CR-C-5: Enrollment-token concurrent TOCTOU** — two concurrent requests with the same token both see `count === 0` before either insert, allowing a double enroll; bounded by `MAX_DEVICES` cap (second insert attempts to exceed cap or hits a unique-constraint conflict); architectural fix needs an atomic check-and-lock pattern. Deferred, bounded risk. Re-trigger: enrollment hardening or multi-device story.

## Deferred from: code review of 1-9-admin-authentication (Group D — Tests, 2026-06-12)

Closure-language posture per [[feedback_closure_language_precision]]: test correctness patches are **Closed by [edit]**; coverage gaps with infrastructure prerequisites are **Resolved via explicit deferral** (gap intentional, re-trigger recorded).

- **CR-D-1: AC-3 session-rotation assertions absent** — no test captures the session cookie before and after any auth-state transition and asserts they differ; testing requires raw `app.inject()` cookie comparison or a `makeClient` redesign; fixation defense is deployed but untested end-to-end. Re-trigger: test-infrastructure story or `makeClient` extension.
- **CR-D-2: AC-7 rate-limit fires — zero coverage** — `TEST_ENV` sets all rate-limit ceilings to 100 000 to prevent 429 interference; no test sets a low ceiling and asserts 429 fires at threshold for login or step-up request. Re-trigger: a per-test env-override pattern (e.g. `testConfig({ LOGIN_RATE_MAX: '3' })`) plus dedicated `rate-limit.spec.ts`.
- **CR-D-3: AC-8 CSRF negative test absent** — all test clients inject valid `origin: TEST_ORIGIN` unconditionally; no test sends a mismatched or absent Origin header to a state-changing route and asserts 403. Re-trigger: dedicated `csrf.spec.ts` or first Story adding more state-changing routes.
- **CR-D-4: AC-1 lockout time-based unlock** — test confirms account locks but never advances a clock past `lockoutMs` and verifies login succeeds again; requires clock injection in the HTTP E2E layer (clock currently not threaded through `requireAdminSession`). Re-trigger: ops hardening story or test-infrastructure clock-injection story.
- **CR-D-5: Enrollment token single-use after device enrolled** — no test sends the same enrollment token after the first device is successfully enrolled and asserts rejection. Re-trigger: next story touching enrollment or a fast-follow test PR.
- **CR-D-6: 7-day absolute session expiry enforcement** — no test advances the clock past `sessionAbsoluteMs` and asserts a protected route returns 401; requires `SESSION_ABSOLUTE_MS` override to a short value + frozen clock in E2E. Re-trigger: test-infrastructure clock injection in E2E.
- **CR-D-7: WebAuthn challenge cleared on failure retry (C-7 path)** — no test attempts `authenticate/verify` a second time after a first failure and confirms the challenge is gone (stale-challenge retry rejected). Re-trigger: next auth hardening story.

Story 1.5 commits Terraform `lifecycle { prevent_destroy = true }` + `destroy_scheduled_duration = "2592000s"` 30-day Cloud KMS max substrate; substantive two-person-approval workflow + paging signal land at **operations policy authoring window** (post-Phase-1-soft-launch); operational territory.

- **D9-1.5: Per-Pariwar HMAC key derivation policy substantive choice** — architecture §2.7 line 1512-1513 ("different keys per Pariwar to prevent cross-Pariwar correlation (where required)"). Story 1.5 substrate defaults to **Option B** (HMAC-input-context-binding via `pariwar:<id>|<field-class>:<plaintext>` prefix on a single KMS HMAC key; cryptographic-level separation; cheaper at KMS-resource level). Substantive choice between Option A (substantively-separate per-Pariwar KMS HMAC keys) vs Option B at production time is **Story 1.6** territory (RLS adversarial test + multi-tenancy substrate). Story 1.6 records the choice in an ADR-NNNN-per-pariwar-hmac-key-policy slot (to be created at Story 1.6 dev-time).
- **D10-1.5: Audit-log entry on every encrypt/decrypt operation** — architecture-implied (no explicit line citation; defense-in-depth for §1.5 audit-log integrity + §2.7 PII tier discipline). The Story 1.5 `KmsProvider` interface includes an optional `auditHook?: (op, kekRef, ctx) => void` slot that Story 1.10 substrate populates; Story 1.5 commits the seam; substantive audit-log entry-writing at **Story 1.10**.
- **D11-1.5: Branded `PariwarId` substituted into `EncryptionContext`** — Story 1.5 substrate uses `pariwarId: string` per `EncryptionContext` shape; Story 1.7 substantively brands. Cross-link to Story 1.4 D12-1.4 (branded ID Story 1.7 trigger). **DEFERRED at Story 1.7 (Resolved via explicit deferral)**: the branded `PariwarId` now exists (D12-1.4 closed) and is used end-to-end in the new Passport code, but substituting it into `EncryptionContext.pariwarId` (and the `computeHmac` context) ripples through `fake-kms-provider`/`cloud-kms-provider`/`blind-index`/`canonical-context` + all 4 encryption test files (which build contexts from raw UUID string literals). Story 1.7 Task 1.3 explicitly authorises skipping the broad retroactive substitution when it balloons scope. Re-trigger: a future encryption-surface story (or a cheap follow-up) brands `EncryptionContext.pariwarId`. AC-5 is satisfied without it. Decision 2026-06-11-043.
- **D12-1.5: Object storage tier policy ADR co-trigger** — `docs/knowledge-transfer/adr-index.md` line 78 `ADR-NNNN-object-storage-tier-policy` close trigger "Story 1.5 (Cloud KMS HSM + Google Tink envelope encryption + PII tiers) + Story 9.2 closure". The ADR substantively lands at **Story 9.2 closure** since the policy spans both PII encryption tiers AND bank statement object storage (KYC docs, death certificates, Contribution Note PDFs, bank statements lifecycle). Story 1.5 contributes the PII-tier-encryption substrate; Story 9.2 contributes the bank-statement object-storage substrate; the ADR substantively author-able only when both substrates exist.
- **D13-1.5: Canonical-JSON consolidation across @twt/events + @twt/domain (deviation-derived).** Story 1.5 dev-story discovered that `@twt/events` (which exports `canonicalJsonStringify`) already depends on `@twt/domain` (via `events-log.ts` importing schema); adding `@twt/events` as a dep of `@twt/domain` for AAD canonical-JSON would create a circular workspace dependency. Resolution: a scoped helper `encryptionContextAad` in `packages/domain/src/encryption/canonical-context.ts` produces RFC 8785 JCS canonical-JSON for the `EncryptionContext` shape only (3 string keys; byte-identical to `canonicalJsonStringify` for this shape). Trigger for consolidation: when a third consumer needs canonical-JSON outside of `@twt/events` AND the cycle becomes a felt-friction (likely Story 1.10+ audit-log hash-chain or Story 7.x snapshot-hash). Consolidation paths to evaluate at that time: (a) move `canonical-json.ts` from `@twt/events` to `@twt/domain` + re-export from events (Story 1.3 substrate modification); (b) introduce a new `@twt/canonical-json` workspace consumed by both events + domain (new workspace addition); (c) accept the duplication and lock the scoped helper into the encryption substrate (current Story 1.5 substrate posture). Decision deferred until concrete third-consumer pressure exists.
- **D14-1.5: Drizzle auto-encrypt-on-write inside `customType` (deviation-derived).** Story 1.5 dev-story verified that `drizzle-orm@^0.45.0`'s `customType` `toDriver` / `fromDriver` callbacks are **synchronous** (not `Promise`-returning). Cloud KMS round-trips are async; making `piiColumn` auto-encrypt-on-write inside `customType` requires either Drizzle 0.46+ async `customType` support OR a synchronous KMS provider variant. Story 1.5 substrate commits `piiColumn` as a TEXT customType with tier metadata attached + `AsyncLocalStorage`-backed `encryptionContextStorage` + `withEncryptionContext` wrapper + service-layer helpers (`encryptTier1` / `decryptTier1` / `blindIndex`) for explicit encrypt-on-write + decrypt-on-read. The integration test demonstrates the pattern end-to-end at the service-layer-helper level. Triggers: (a) Drizzle 0.46+ release with async `customType` support — flip the substrate to auto-encrypt-inside-customType; (b) Story 1.9+ `apps/api` substantive-route landing — wire the Fastify pre-handler hook to populate `encryptionContextStorage` and standardise the service-layer-helper invocation convention. Anticipated in the Story 1.5 file Dev Notes "Drizzle column-transformer pattern" fallback (b).

- **No tooling for cross-reference link checking** [`credential-inventory.md` owning-Story citations] — inventory cites Stories 1.2, 1.5, 1.10, 1.13, 1.15, 3.3b, 7.7, 9.2, 10.3, 12.x, 14.3, 0.4, 0.6, 0.13 by name. Story renumbering risk is real. CI link-checker gate deferred to Story 1.16a.

- **Redundant-custody sync drift** [`README.md` candidate mechanisms 3+4] — if the ADR selects two custodial locations for redundancy, the sync discipline is the ADR's responsibility. - **`.decision-log.md` supersession enforcement convention-only** — file's "never edit in place" rule is convention; no CI/hook prevents in-place edits of ratified decisions. Inherited from Story 0.1 schema; cross-Story coordination needed.
- **Status vocab drift (`Ratified` vs `Trustee-ratified`)** — `.decision-log.md` file template lists `Ratified` while Story 0.2 spec Dev Notes + sealing-procedure §2.7 use `Trustee-ratified`. The file template was Story 0.1's; patching template is a Story 0.1 amendment, not Story 0.2 scope. Worth aligning when Story 0.1 next sees a touch.
- **Audit-line emission citations interpretive** — `sealing-procedure.md` §2.7 cites architecture §1.5 + §2.10 as the source for "sealing event emits an audit line"; the cites are interpretive rather than a literal commitment in those sections. Worth architectural cross-check before trustee sign-off but not a clean patch.

Row hedges correctly with "confirm with Trustee Panel during Task 7"; the hedge is the right closure-language posture. Not patchable without trustee involvement.

Scaffolding scope is undecided (Story 0.2 Task 6 with explicit agreement, OR Story 0.15, OR a dedicated bootstrap story).
